- **kmp**: Kotlin Multiplatform patterns and architecture. Use when working with shared code, source sets, expect/actual declarations, or project configuration. ([pddhkt/kotlin-kmp-skills](https://github.com/pddhkt/kotlin-kmp-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/kotlin-kmp/skills/kmp) · ★1)
- **kmp**: Use ONLY when configuring KMP targets, source-set hierarchies, or `expect`/`actual` placement in a module INSIDE the SKaiNET repository. Trigger tokens include `kotlin { ... }`, `iosArm64()`, `commonMain`, `jvmMain`, `expect fun`, `actual fun`, `androidNative`, `wasmJs`, source-set dependency edits in `SKaiNET/skainet-*/build.gradle.kts`. Do NOT fire on KMP setup in a CONSUMER project (an app that depends on SKaiNET) — those concerns are simpler and live in `skainet-consumer-setup`. ([SKaiNET-developers/SKaiNET-coding-skills](https://github.com/SKaiNET-developers/SKaiNET-coding-skills/tree/HEAD/skainet-contributor-skills/skills/kmp))

- **kmp-android-cli**: KMP-specific integration guide for Google's official `android` CLI.
